# Chapter 10-a — Group H: C-8

**Document:** `NH_MASTER-21_SYSTEM_BEHAVIOR_v0_1_CANDIDATE__CH10-a.md`  
**Status:** CANDIDATE  
**Source repository:** `nesgeva/NH-GOVERNANCE`  
**Source commit:** `6a7160ba688ba4e433a31899162815df7e2bab17`  

This piece covers the research component, source preservation and restricted review, accepted B13 isolation and duplicate/recovery mechanics, and the specifically restored research rule and bounded historical values. Existing Catalog, store, retry, privacy, access and world-model owners retain their canonical cards. The general model/kernel scope remains CH10-b, wellbeing CH10-c, interface CH10-e, the connected research path CH11 and final registers CH12. B13 completion does not complete B-CYCLE-2 or choose the capture technology, query-cap value or present-day schedule.

[SOURCE CONFLICT: DD §3I and §5 and COMP §8 / ACADEMIC SOURCE leave Semantic Scholar versus OpenAlex versus both open. V10 §8 selects both. The V10 selection is retained; the older open-choice text is not adopted.]

[SOURCE CONFLICT: DD §3I requires corroboration for important claims from incompletely preserved sources, and COMP §8 / FALLBACK lowers their evidential confidence and requires corroboration. DD §5 also calls the screenshot's inert-image role undecided. V10 §8 makes the screenshot a human visual-review copy only, never a reasoning source; its absence neither lowers textual evidence confidence nor automatically requires corroboration. V10 controls all three points.]

[SOURCE CONFLICT: The inherited V10 §2 versus §7B wording conflict remains applicable to the C-8 use at C-7B.8. Its existing named-gap handling is preserved; this piece neither resolves that conflict nor edits the earlier owner.]

### C-8 — Research Pipeline / Knowledge Catcher (§8)
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §8] [MAP C-8]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The outside-information gathering and stored-information currency-checking component. [V10 §8]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — Research topics, raw outside sources and stored information to compare against outside evidence. [V10 §8]
- Does: DESIGNED — Runs raw fetch, one auditable text synthesis, create-space/gate and review before authorized memory entry. Research knowledge grows memory as readings and never trains the mouth. [V10 §8] [MAP C-8]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — Review candidates, retained rejections, preserved sources and authorized research readings; currency checks distinguish still supported, outdated, uncertain and contradicted information. [V10 §8]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Let fetched instructions command N.H, promote an injected synthesis directly into REALITY or an action, train the mouth, or grant itself a re-check schedule. [V10 §8]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Keeps isolation-uncertain or integrity-failed material outside the queue, records and reports the condition, and permits no progress toward memory. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

### C-8.2 — Raw research fetch
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §8]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — Brave Search API raw JSON from its independent index, separate from the synthesis service. [V10 §8]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — A search query. [V10 §8]
- Does: DESIGNED — Retrieves titles, URLs and snippets without an upstream AI extraction, relevance-ranking or content-validation layer. Any needed full-page text is fetched as plain text, never executed. [V10 §8]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — Raw source titles, URLs and text snippets for the separate synthesis step. [V10 §8]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Download and execute source content or treat arbitrary page JavaScript as part of the snippet-fetch path. Brave and OpenRouter keys serve different jobs; replacing search does not remove the synthesis key. [V10 §8]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Records a failed fetch honestly, with its retryable or terminal classification; fabricates no replacement content. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

### C-8.3 — Single research synthesis
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §8]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — The one auditable OpenRouter/llama synthesis between raw search and review. [V10 §8]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — Unverified raw web text returned by search. [V10 §8]
- Does: DESIGNED — Extracts source-grounded material as a text-only synthesis for create-space/gate. The system prompt explicitly treats search results as data from which to extract facts, never as instructions. [V10 §8]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — A synthesis that remains unverified and can enter SIMULATION/review, never directly cause an action or write to REALITY. [V10 §8]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Call tools, access files, initiate outbound requests or follow instructions embedded in fetched text. [V10 §8]

### C-8.12 — Screenshot-unavailable preservation fallback
Stamp: DESIGNED    Source: [V10 §8]

ALONE
- What it is: DESIGNED — SOURCE PRESERVATION INCOMPLETE when the full-page screenshot cannot be preserved. [V10 §8]
- Takes in: DESIGNED — Saved text source and excerpt, inactive URL, provenance metadata, timestamp, research-reading ID, integrity hash and the known capture-failure reason. [V10 §8]
- Does: DESIGNED — Retains all those materials, marks the preservation failure clearly and allows reasoning from verified saved text and source metadata. Screenshot absence neither lowers textual evidential confidence nor automatically creates a corroboration requirement. [V10 §8]
- Gives out: DESIGNED — Usable text evidence with an honest preservation-incomplete status and known failure reason. [V10 §8]
- Must never: DESIGNED — Discard automatically, hide the preservation failure, claim a complete screenshot, or penalize text evidence merely because the human-review image is missing. [V10 §8]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Text, metadata, provenance or integrity failure still fails closed; this fallback excuses only the screenshot's absence. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

ALONE

- Takes in: ACCEPTED — Outside research material and the source-preservation artifacts. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — An unverifiable context boundary or preservation integrity failure keeps the material outside the queue, records and reports the failure, and stops its progress toward memory. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

### C-8.16 — Research retry and committed recovery
Stamp: ACCEPTED    Source: [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]

ALONE
- What it is: ACCEPTED — Research's consumption of the accepted operation/retry spine. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]
- Takes in: ACCEPTED — Retryable or terminal stage failures and committed per-stage progress. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]
- Does: ACCEPTED — Applies exactly 3 total technical attempts, waiting at least 10 then 30 seconds live or 1 then 3 minutes background/nightly, with elapsed ceilings of 7 or 15 minutes respectively. Both attempt and elapsed gates apply; the first to close stops retry. A bad, unsafe or unsupported proposal has exactly 1 careful retry after durable rejection. Early stop remains allowed when retry is useless or unsafe; further continuation requires a recorded real change, never repetition alone. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3]
- Gives out: ACCEPTED — Bounded retry, an honest terminal halt, or recovery from the last durable checkpoint. Completed items stand and unfinished items resume. Every recovery action is itself a recorded operation. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3]
- Must never: ACCEPTED — Reset bounds through replay, reconstruct uncommitted work, silently rerun completed work or claim a failed stage complete. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3] [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §5]
- Fails closed by: ACCEPTED — Terminal failure halts; uncertainty admits nothing to memory, activates nothing and claims no completion. [04/NH_BUNDLE_6_MECHANICAL_DESIGN_v1_4_CANDIDATE.md §3]

TOGETHER
